        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Sloop, traversing large sized bodies of water should be relatively easy. To make sure you dont end up at the mercy of the wind you can lower your Anchor to stop in place.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You can also dye your sails with dye of your choice!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the task of this quest, you need to look at any finished Sloop.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Tip: Try not to sail through rivers with a sloop, it will likely NOT work as expected!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Where's the Wind Waker when you need it?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.lookat} #alekiships:sloops",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Sloop",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Rowboat, you can now transport a decent amount of items across the oceans, perfect for overseas exploration, perhaps to the tropics! Make sure you create two Oars so you can go faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You can also dye it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "To complete the first task of this quest, you need to look at any finished Rowboat.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Tips: Two passenger slots? Perfect for bringing home pairs of breeding animals for your farm through a river system or across lakes and oceans.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Happy Rowboat-ing!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.lookat} #alekiships:rowboats",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Complete the Rowboat",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "With the Kayak in hand, you can now enjoy your quick solo journeys on water. Make sure you craft a Kayak Paddle (the canoe one won't work!) to go even faster!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",

          "text": "Happy Kayak-ing!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Canoe",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Kayak is the second most simple boat you can craft, requiring Waxes for the Waterproof Hides. It is also the only boat that can be picked up as an item. Despite having a single passenger slot, it is the fastest boat in the game until the steel age Sloop!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",

          "key": "quest-snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        },
        {

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Kayak",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Requiring more complex materials, locking it to the bronze age, the Rowboat has capacity for 2 passengers and 2 storage, or 4 storage with no passengers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "You'll need to find some sort of Hardwood, detailed in the Field Guide, which also includes the construction process.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Keep in mind the boat must be constructed with the same wood! Do not mix different kinds of hardwoods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:firmaciv/rowboat",

          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "Get ready for some work now.",

          "label": "tips__transportation",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",

        },
        {

        {

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Rowboat",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/tips__transportation",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/tips__transportation.snbt",
          "text": "The Sloop is the most advanced seaworthy vessel, requiring Wrought Iron. It has ample space for storage, anvils and workbenches! It is incredibly expensive and requires practice to sail. The Field Guide has all the details for construction and on how to sail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "tips__transportation",

        },

          "text": "Again, do not attempt to mix up different kinds of hardwoods in the same vessel.",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "What is the Beneath?",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "The Beneath is a network of very deep caves, directly below the Overworld. This dimension is balanced around mid to late Primitive Age. It's more dangerous than the surface, so we recommend a full set of bronze armor and a metal shield as a minimum, as well as plenty of food, water, and other tools.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "It differs from the usual Nether experience in a few ways: - You enter it differently - Water doesn't instantly evaporate - A bed works like normal - Traveling Beneath is no faster than in the Overworld In return, you can find resources like Kaolin Clay, Graphite, richer and more exposed ore veins, as well as other treasures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "Entering the Beneath",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "To travel Beneath, simply stand on overworld bedrock for a little while. To get down to the bedrock, you'll have to either break through the Hornfels layer with Explosives or Steel+ Tools, or find a cave with the more brittle Keratophyre.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "To return, stand directly below ceiling bedrock in the same fashion. It's a good idea to make a note of where you entered, so you can return to the same place in the overworld whenever you're ready to leave.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "The Beneath is a network of very deep caves, directly below the Overworld. This dimension is balanced around mid to late Primitive Age. It's more dangerous than the surface, so we recommend a full set of bronze armor and a metal shield as a minimum, as well as plenty of food, water, and other tools.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "It differs from the usual Nether experience in a few ways: - You enter it differently - Water doesn't instantly evaporate - A bed works like normal - Traveling Beneath is no faster than in the Overworld In return, you can find resources like Kaolin Clay, Graphite, richer and more exposed ore veins, as well as other treasures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "Entering the Beneath",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "To travel Beneath, simply stand on overworld bedrock for a little while. To get down to the bedrock, you'll have to either break through the Hornfels layer with Explosives or Steel+ Tools, or find a cave with the more brittle Keratophyre.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "To return, stand directly below ceiling bedrock in the same fashion. It's a good idea to make a note of where you entered, so you can return to the same place in the overworld whenever you're ready to leave.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "minecraft:deepslate",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "(SiO₂)₄(KMg₃Al₃F₂Si₃O₁₀)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Raw Migmatite",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        }

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "First, like the Moon, Mars will have some hostile surface mobs as well. If you haven't already figured out how to make your Space Suit stay with you after dying or invested in new EV-tier weaponry, you'll have a rough time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The planet is mostly a desert world, dotted with \"islands\" where you’ll find water, lush fauna, and much safer places to build your first base. Meanwhile, the open desert is extremely dangerous if you don't watch your step, but contains some other crucial resources!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "All fluid veins are spread across Mars, except for Heavy Ammoniacial Water which can only be found outside of the desert. That’s another good reason to set up your base there.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Energy-wise, Mars will not be kind to you at first.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Nearly every common energy generation method is disabled or worthless on the planet, which means you’ll have to rely almost entirely on Fission to power your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "At the very beginning, we recommend bringing along some filled batteries such as Lapotron Crystals to get started, and then look towards setting up Thorium Fuel Rods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Achieving energy stability on Mars will take time and effort, but once your systems are running smoothly, you’ll feel like unlimited power is right in the palm of your hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "And one last tip: don’t kill everything you see. Some animals can be ranched, and you’ll definitely need them later in your progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to check out the Space Survival chapter for more Mars-related tips too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Stay alert, plan ahead, and Mars will reward your courage.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Red Planet",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Land on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "I'm on Mars!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The water on Mars is Semiheavy which means it contains one Deuterium atom, and is also full of Ammonia, a great antifreeze, which is how it can stay liquid despite the very low temperatures.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "It's technically drinkable, but you'll likely want to distill it into normal Water first. You can also move source blocks of Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water around and get more of it via an Aqueous Accumulator, just like regular Water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also centrifuge the Ammonium Chloride out from it and electrolyze it as a source of Nitrogen. Easy Nitrox from just one source!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Fancy a drink?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Dotted around the vast Martian deserts are small \"islands\" of life and vegetation, which are probably where you'll want to set up your base. Surviving here will be much easier than in the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is that a dinosaur?!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Jungles",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The majority of Mars is covered in a vast desert. Not much lives here except the occasional Stackatick, and of course the giant Sandworms. If this is your first time here, you'll want to find somewhere more hospitable! Otherwise, you'll want to keep searching...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Sand, sand everywhere",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Visit the Martian Deep Desert biome",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Desert",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },

        {

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "First, like the Moon, Mars will have some hostile surface mobs as well. If you haven't already figured out how to make your Space Suit stay with you after dying or invested in new EV-tier weaponry, you'll have a rough time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The planet is mostly a desert world, dotted with \"islands\" where you’ll find water, lush fauna, and much safer places to build your first base. Meanwhile, the open desert is extremely dangerous if you don't watch your step, but contains some other crucial resources!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "All fluid veins are spread across Mars, except for Heavy Ammoniacial Water which can only be found outside of the desert. That’s another good reason to set up your base there.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Energy-wise, Mars will not be kind to you at first.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Nearly every common energy generation method is disabled or worthless on the planet, which means you’ll have to rely almost entirely on Fission to power your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "At the very beginning, we recommend bringing along some filled batteries such as Lapotron Crystals to get started, and then look towards setting up Thorium Fuel Rods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Achieving energy stability on Mars will take time and effort, but once your systems are running smoothly, you’ll feel like unlimited power is right in the palm of your hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "And one last tip: don’t kill everything you see. Some animals can be ranched, and you’ll definitely need them later in your progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to check out the Space Survival chapter for more Mars-related tips too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Stay alert, plan ahead, and Mars will reward your courage.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Red Planet",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Land on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "I'm on Mars!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The water on Mars is Semiheavy which means it contains one Deuterium atom, and is also full of Ammonia, a great antifreeze, which is how it can stay liquid despite the very low temperatures.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "It's technically drinkable, but you'll likely want to distill it into normal Water first. You can also move source blocks of Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water around and get more of it via an Aqueous Accumulator, just like regular Water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also centrifuge the Ammonium Chloride out from it and electrolyze it as a source of Nitrogen. Easy Nitrox from just one source!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Fancy a drink?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Dotted around the vast Martian deserts are small \"islands\" of life and vegetation, which are probably where you'll want to set up your base. Surviving here will be much easier than in the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is that a dinosaur?!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Jungles",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The majority of Mars is covered in a vast desert. Not much lives here except the occasional Stackatick, and of course the giant Sandworms. If this is your first time here, you'll want to find somewhere more hospitable! Otherwise, you'll want to keep searching...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Sand, sand everywhere",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Visit the Martian Deep Desert biome",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Desert",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },

        {

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "First, like the Moon, Mars will have some hostile surface mobs as well. If you haven't already figured out how to make your Space Suit stay with you after dying or invested in new EV-tier weaponry, you'll have a rough time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The planet is mostly a desert world, dotted with \"islands\" where you’ll find water, lush fauna, and much safer places to build your first base. Meanwhile, the open desert is extremely dangerous if you don't watch your step, but contains some other crucial resources!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "All fluid veins are spread across Mars, except for Heavy Ammoniacial Water which can only be found outside of the desert. That’s another good reason to set up your base there.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Energy-wise, Mars will not be kind to you at first.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Nearly every common energy generation method is disabled or worthless on the planet, which means you’ll have to rely almost entirely on Fission to power your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "At the very beginning, we recommend bringing along some filled batteries such as Lapotron Crystals to get started, and then look towards setting up Thorium Fuel Rods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Achieving energy stability on Mars will take time and effort, but once your systems are running smoothly, you’ll feel like unlimited power is right in the palm of your hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "And one last tip: don’t kill everything you see. Some animals can be ranched, and you’ll definitely need them later in your progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to check out the Space Survival chapter for more Mars-related tips too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Stay alert, plan ahead, and Mars will reward your courage.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Red Planet",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Land on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "I'm on Mars!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The water on Mars is Semiheavy which means it contains one Deuterium atom, and is also full of Ammonia, a great antifreeze, which is how it can stay liquid despite the very low temperatures.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "It's technically drinkable, but you'll likely want to distill it into normal Water first. You can also move source blocks of Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water around and get more of it via an Aqueous Accumulator, just like regular Water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also centrifuge the Ammonium Chloride out from it and electrolyze it as a source of Nitrogen. Easy Nitrox from just one source!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Fancy a drink?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Dotted around the vast Martian deserts are small \"islands\" of life and vegetation, which are probably where you'll want to set up your base. Surviving here will be much easier than in the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is that a dinosaur?!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Jungles",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The majority of Mars is covered in a vast desert. Not much lives here except the occasional Stackatick, and of course the giant Sandworms. If this is your first time here, you'll want to find somewhere more hospitable! Otherwise, you'll want to keep searching...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Sand, sand everywhere",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Visit the Martian Deep Desert biome",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Desert",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "What is the Beneath?",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "The Beneath is a network of very deep caves, directly below the Overworld. This dimension is balanced around mid to late Primitive Age. It's more dangerous than the surface, so we recommend a full set of bronze armor and a metal shield as a minimum, as well as plenty of food, water, and other tools.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "It differs from the usual Nether experience in a few ways: - You enter it differently - Water doesn't instantly evaporate - A bed works like normal - Traveling Beneath is no faster than in the Overworld In return, you can find resources like Kaolin Clay, Graphite, richer and more exposed ore veins, as well as other treasures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "Entering the Beneath",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "To travel Beneath, simply stand on overworld bedrock for a little while. To get down to the bedrock, you'll have to either break through the Hornfels layer with Explosives or Steel+ Tools, or find a cave with the more brittle Keratophyre.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/beneath.json",
          "text": "To return, stand directly below ceiling bedrock in the same fashion. It's a good idea to make a note of where you entered, so you can return to the same place in the overworld whenever you're ready to leave.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "What is the Beneath?",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Hydrogen is an essential component of countless MV+ chemical processes, such as acids or ammonia.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in infinite amounts by electrolyzing water or by reacting water with the Formic Acid loop.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Deuterium and Tritium are used in LuV for fusion and can be obtained by centrifuging hydrogen or electrolyzing heavy water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:deuterium\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:deuterium\"}})item(gtceu:deuterium_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:tritium\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:tritium\"}})item(gtceu:tritium_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Helium is used in HV+ to cool down hot ingots in the vacuum freezer and to speed up the EBF.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in HV from Liquid Earth Air distillation and with the Fluid Drilling Rig on the moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Helium-3 is used for recipes on the Moon and can be obtained by a Fluid Drilling Rig on the Moon or by centrifuging helium.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:helium\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:helium\"}})item(gtceu:helium_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
