        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Helium 3",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Lithium is the best electrolyte for LV, MV, and HV batteries.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It is abundant in Tungstate and Scheelite veins. The tungsten line yields lithium as a byproduct.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #3",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Beryllium is widely used for nuclear fission, including fissile fuel rods, Heat Batteries, and fuels such as FLiBe. It is also used in HV to create Ender Dust.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained from emerald veins.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #4",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Boron is a component of borosilicate glass used in MV+ to make gem lenses and in HV+ to make AE2 cables, Vitrified Ender Pearls, and Fiber-Reinforced Circuit Boards.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in MV from Borax.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #5",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Carbon is the base of organic chemistry. It is used in LV+ to make resistors and in HV+ to make carbon fibers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in large amounts from diamond and in MV in infinite amounts from oil and tar distillation.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #6",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Nitrogen is used in LV+ for countless chemical reactions and to speed up the EBF and pyrolyse oven.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in infinite amounts from air centrifuging",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.chemical_elements.element} #7",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:nitrogen\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:nitrogen\"}})item(gtceu:nitrogen_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Oxygen is used in LV+ for countless chemical reactions and to speed up the EBF.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in LV in infinite amounts from air centrifuging, water electrolysis, and the extremely potent flint dust to silicon dioxide processing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "or(nbt(fuzzy:{fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:oxygen\"}})nbt(fuzzy:{Fluid:{FluidName:\"gtceu:oxygen\"}})item(gtceu:oxygen_bucket))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "Fluorine is used in HV+ for polytetrafluoroethylene.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/chemical_elements",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/chemical_elements.snbt",
          "text": "It can be obtained in MV from electrolyzing various ores such as Topaz. Unlike other gaseous elements, fluorine cannot be obtained in infinite amounts, but Topaz and Bastnatite is fluorine-rich and abundant on the Moon.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "chemical_elements",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

        },
        {

        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Soul soil may be tilled to form Soul Farmland. Crops work a little differently when you are Beneath. There are five nutrients: Death, Destruction, Decay, Sorrow, and Flame. Nutrients are viewed by holding a hoe, but will not appear unless they have a value greater than zero.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crops have a preferred nutrient: Instead of consuming it, this is the nutrient they will add to over time. The crop will happily consume the other four nutrients in order to grow. Nether crops cannot die, but still need block light to grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are as follows: Sulfur: 20% decay, 10% flame Gunpowder: 60% Destruction, 10% Flame, 40% Death Ghast Tear: 30% Sorrow Blaze Powder: 20% Flame",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The fertilizers are also available in obtainable 'pure' forms from chest loot. Crafting these together yields Agonizing Fertilizer, which has them all in a neat package.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Crimson Roots (Decay) and Warped Roots (Destruction) are growable as crops. These are most useful as means of improving the nutrient quality of your soul farmland. Nether Wart (Death), found in fortresses, can also be grown as a crop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "The Gleamflower (Flame) can be grown as a crop, and also found in the world as a plant. It is useful as an infinite light source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops",
          "source": "jar:beneath-1.20.1-1.0.6.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/crops.json",
          "text": "Ghost Peppers (Sorrow) are an edible vegetable that can be grown in the Beneath!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crops",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 7

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "A Crucible is an advanced device used for the creation of Alloys. It is a more precise method than using a Small Vessel to make alloys. To obtain a crucible, you will first need to obtain some Fire Clay, which is a stronger material than clay. This fire clay can then be knapped to shape it into an Unfired Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "Knapping an Unfired Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "After the crucible is knapped, it will need to be fired, like any piece of pottery - a Pit Kiln or Charcoal Forge would do. In order to use the crucible, it needs a source of heat. The crucible can be heated by any heatable block below, usually a Charcoal Forge",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "A crucible heated by a charcoal forge below it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "Now, you are ready to use the crucible. When you use it, you will see the Crucible Interface, shown to the right. The top region shows the current metal content of the crucible. The first metal shown is what would be produced, if it were to be extracted right now. Other metals shown are the makeup of the current alloy in the crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "Advanced Alloying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "The Crucible Interface",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "The crucible has nine slots where items can be added to be melted, and their liquefied contents will be directly added to the crucible. Molten metal containers such as Molds can also be placed here and they will be slowly drained into the crucible, allowing for precise control over your alloy's content. Holding while hovering over a draining mold or vessel makes it drain faster. Molds or other fluid containers can also be placed in the output slot, and will be slowly filled with the current content of the crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "The temperature indicator on the left will rise based on external sources of heat, such as a Charcoal Forge below or heat from a Blast Furnace from above. Metal can be extracted from the crucible as long as it is still molten. Finally, the crucible will keep its contents when broken, allowing you to transport the alloy container around if you wish.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge.json",
          "text": "Charcoal Forge",
          "key": "guide-page:name",

          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge.json",
          "text": "The Charcoal Forge is a device used to heat and melt items. Forges are necessary to make a crucible work. They are typically used to heat items to prepare them for smithing on an anvil. It is constructed with 5 stone blocks surrounding a charcoal pile of 7 or 8 layers which is then lit.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Forge",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge.json",
          "text": "A Charcoal Forge",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.name",
          "label": "Charcoal Forge",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge.json",
          "text": "A complete forge multiblock, ready to be lit.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Forge",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge.json",
          "text": "A Forge needs a chimney in order to work. The block directly above the Forge must be air, or a Crucible. In addition, either the block directly above, or one of eight nearby blocks must be able to see the sky - the blue stained glass in the visualization to the right. A Bellows can be placed on one block up and adjacent to the Forge, in order to increase the maximum temperature it can reach.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Forge",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge.json",
          "text": "The forge's interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Forge",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/charcoal_forge.json",
          "text": "The five slots at the bottom of the forge are used for fuel. While the forge is running, it periodically consumes fuel, which can be Charcoal or Coal. The top five slots will heat items, up to the temperature shown in the indicator on the left. The right side slots are for containers that can contain liquid metal, like Vessels and Molds. Items that melt in the forge will fill those containers.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Charcoal Forge",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "count": 6
        },
